Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the algebraic structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 366. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 271 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_366(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 366. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^271(S, F) is trivial. QED.
